LP iteration steps:
function lp_step(f, M, t steps):
- Normalize f sum=1 (copy). M = max conv(f,f) (normalized small ~0.78/n).
- For each iteration (maybe 1): build C matrix: C[k, i] = f[k-i] if 0<=k-i<n else 0. For k=0..2n-2.
- Bounds g≥0, maybe ub=10? Since normalized h sum1, g sum can be up to? Individual g bound not needed but to avoid inf. M/f0 maybe ~√n*? For n=1024 ~30. Set ub=10n? But large LP.
- c_obj = ones(n). Solve linprog(c_obj, A_ub=C, b_ub=M*ones, bounds=(0,ub)). The objective max sum. Since no sum constraint, optimum finite due bounds? If ub too high, LP may push to ub for many variables but constraints limit. We can omit ub (None) if constraints ensure finite? For g variables, each column j has constraints for k=j (f0 g_j) and maybe k=n-1+j (f_{n-1}g_j) etc. f0>0 so finite if f0>0. If f has zeros, rows with all zeros? Then unbounded. Ensure f has small positive floor (add 1e-300?) But f0? We can add epsilon to sequence before. Use ub=1e6 to be safe.
- res.x = g. Normalize h=y/sum if sum>0.
- Evaluate score h. Take best among t in mix (0,1] and maybe h. Since LP objective may not reduce score; we line search.
- Use h as new f for next iteration if improved? The state-of-art says line search nonincreasing. We can accept if score improves by >1e-10. We can also blend.

Let's design lp_iteration(f):
Input f length n, scaled so max_conv(f)=1 (or approximately). Sum S.
- Add tiny floor to f? f*f=1. For building C, values positive.
- A_ub (2n-1,n) as above.
- b_ub = ones(2n-1)
- bounds list [(0,1)]*n.
- Solve linprog(c_obj=ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs') maybe options presolve=True.
- If success: h=res.x; else None.
- Clean h nonfinite, clip 0,1.
- Compute M_h=max_conv(h). If M_h<=0 return.
- h_feas = h / M_h (since M_h maybe >0). This has max≈1 and same score_normalized. Sum S_h=sum(h_feas).

Let's test coordinate candidate formula in detail to avoid bugs:
For given a, c full length L. Coordinate i, old x=a[i].
We want base = convolution with a_i=0.
For all j:
- If j!=i, c_{i+j} contains 2 a_i a_j. There is also contributions not involving i. So base[k] = c[k] - 2 x a_j for k=i+j, j≠i.
- For j=i, c_{2i} contains x^2. base[2i]=c[2i]-x^2.
To compute base:
   base = c.copy()
   lv = np.zeros(L)
   idx = i + np.arange(n)
   lv[idx] = 2.0*a
   # at idx for j=i is 2i, set 0 to avoid subtracting 2x*x
   lv[k2] = 0.0
   base[idx] -= x * lv[idx]  # because 2x*a_j; at k2 subtract 0
   if k2 valid: base[k2] -= x*x
But after `lv[idx] = 2*a`, if k2 also equals i+j for some other j? 2i only j=i, so fine.
For candidate y:
   c_y = base + y * lv + (y^2 at k2)

def power_seq(n, alpha, beta, eps=1e-6):
   i=np.arange(n)
   left=(i+1)**(-alpha)
   right=(n-i)**(-beta)  # at last 1
   a=left*right
   # ensure nonnegative
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-1.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a = central_seq(512)
    best_score = score_array(best_a)
    # use height_sequence_1 if valid
    # evaluate central lengths
    for n in (128,256,512,1024,2048):
       a=central_seq(n); sc=score_array(a); update
    # Evaluate some beta shapes / noisy for lengths [256,512,1024]
       maybe update.
    # LP search for selected lengths
    for n in (256,512,1024):
       if time left < threshold break
       start_a = resample(best_a,n) maybe if length not n else copy; also central
       # Initialize candidate for LP as best among central/resample/noisy for this n
       a0 = ... scaled M=1
       for attempt in range(max_attempts):
           # try LP from current a0
           a_new, sc_new = lp_step(a0)
           if improved: update global and a0; continue attempts
           else break
       # coordinate polish a0
       a_pol, sc_pol = coordinate_polish(a0, budget maybe 20s)
       update
    # L-BFGS polish global best if n<=512 and time
       anchor = argmax(best); run smooth lbfgs; update.
    # anneal? maybe skip or simple coordinate final.
    # final validate, return list

def coordinate_polish(a, budget_s, rng=None, max_sweeps=None):
    a=np.asarray(float); n=a.size
    if n<2 or n>1200: return a, score_array(a)
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
    # normalize M=1 maybe
    a, M = normalize_M(a)
    s=a.sum(); c=convolve_full(a); M=c.max(); score=2n*M/s^2
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    sweeps=0
    order=list(range(n))
    rng pass for shuffle
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and sweeps<max_sweeps:
       if rng is not None: rng.shuffle(order)
       improved_sweep=False
       for i in order:
          if time... break
          new=coordinate_update(a,c,s,M,score,i)
          if improved: update; improved_sweep=True
       # occasional renormalize M=1? score invariant but to keep coordinates, scale by sqrt(1/M)
       M = c.max(); if M>0 and (M>1.0001 or M<0.9999): a = a/math.sqrt(M); c=c/(M); s=s/math.sqrt(M); score=score? invariant; recompute? score same. But a scaled.
       sweeps+=1
       if not improved_sweep:
          # try random perturb and few sweeps maybe break if no improve
          break
    final_score=score_array(best_a)
    return best_a, final_score
```
Need coordinate_update returns new a,s,c,score or None. To avoid copying a each coordinate, we need update c and a in place. But candidate evaluation depends on c and a. If accept, we can modify a[i] and c. The base/lin_vec computed from old. Function can do in-place.
Pseudo:
```
def coordinate_update(a,c,s,M,score,i,n):
   x=a[i]
   k2=2*i; L=2*n-1
   base=c.copy()
   lv=np.zeros(L)
   idx=i+np.arange(n)
   lv[idx]=2*a
   if k2<L: lv[k2]=0.0
   # old x maybe
   base[idx] -= x*lv[idx]
   if k2<L: base[k2] -= x*x
   # candidates
   amax = np.max(a) maybe maintain?
   cands=coordinate_candidates(x, amax, high=5.0)
   Y=np.array(cands)
   # linear max: base[None,:] + Y[:,None]*lv[None,:]
   term = base[None,:] + Y[:,None]*lv[None,:]
   M_lin = term.max(axis=1)
   if k2<L: Mvals = np.maximum(M_lin, base[k2] + Y*Y)
   else Mvals=M_lin
   Svals = s - x + Y
   valid = Svals>1e-12
   F = np.full_like(Y, np.inf)
   F[valid] = (2.0*n)*Mvals[valid]/(Svals[valid]*Svals[valid])
   best_idx=np.argmin(F)
   if F[best_idx] < score - 1e-12:
       y=float(Y[best_idx])
       c = base + y*lv
       if k2<L: c[k2] += y*y
       # clip small neg
       if c.min()<0: c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       a[i]=y
       s = s - x + y
       M = float(c.max())
       score = float(2*n*M/(s*s))
       return True, ...
   return False
```

```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
   n=f.size; L=2*n-1
   A=np.empty((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
   # initialize? np.zeros
   A=np.zeros((L,n))
   for k in range(L):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      if lo<=hi:
         A[k,lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   return A
```

Pseudo:
```
def lp_search_length(n, deadline, rng):
    best_local = central_seq(n) scaled M=1
    best_local_score=score
    # try starts: central, resample global best, noisy central, height seq
    starts=[]
    for each start: scale M=1; if score<best...
    starts sort by score; plus current
    attempts=0
    while time_left and attempts<max_attempts:
       # pick next start: local best if used? We'll use local best for iterations, after stuck add perturbed versions.
       cand, sc = lp_one_step(local_best)
       if cand none break
       if sc < best_local_score:
           best_local = normalize(cand); attempts=0
           print
       else:
           # generate a new anchor: blend with best, add noise
           if no more starts: break
           local_best = next start
    # coordinate polish local best with some time
    return best_local
```

`convolve_full(arr)`:
```
def convolve_full(arr):
    n=arr.size
    L=2*n-1
    if n <= 2048:
        c=np.convolve(arr, arr, 'full')
    else:
        N=1
        while N < L: N <<=1
        fa=np.fft.rfft(arr, N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:L]
        # small imaginary? irfft real; rounding
        c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c
```
But FFT of square: `fa*fa` not `fa*fa.conj()` because convolution not autocorrelation. Correct: linear convolution is inverse FFT of product of DFTs. For real sequences, `irfft(fa*fa)`. Good.
Direct np.convolve for n=2048 maybe memory okay. Could choose threshold 1024? Use direct for <=1500. For n=2048 4M conv, 32MB array maybe? Output length4095 not huge; input 16KB; np.convolve uses direct? It may be O(n^2) but C. 4M ops okay. Use threshold 2048.

def score_array(a):
   try arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   except: return inf
   if arr.size==0: return inf
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): ...
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   c=convolve_full(arr)
   m=c.max()
   if not finite: return inf
   val=2*arr.size*m/(s*s)
   return val if finite else inf
```

LP build dense:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
   n=f.size; L=2*n-1
   A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for k in range(L):
       lo = 0 if k < n else k-n+1
       hi = k if k < n else n-1
       if lo <= hi:
           cnt=hi-lo+1
           A[k, lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   return A
```
Check slice `k-hi:k-lo+1`: If k<n, hi=k, k-hi=0, slice 0:k-lo+1 (lo0) length k+1, reversed [f_k,...,f0]. Good. If k>=n, lo=k-n+1, hi=n-1. k-hi = k-(n-1) = k-n+1 = lo; k-lo+1 = k-(k-n+1)+1 = n. Slice lo:n length n, reversed [f_{n-1},...,f_lo]? For k=n, lo=1,hi=n-1, slice 1:n [f1..f_{n-1}] reversed [f_{n-1}..f1], assignment columns1..n-1; row k=n should be C[n,i]=f[n-i] for i=1..n-1: i=1 f_{n-1}, i=n-1 f_1. Good.

LP solve:
```
def lp_solve_one(f, ub=1.0):
   if linprog is None: return None
   A=build_conv_matrix(f)
   b=np.ones(A.shape[0])
   c_obj=np.ones(f.size)
   bounds=[(0.0,ub)]*f.size
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None: return None
   g=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float).ravel()
   g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
   return np.clip(g,0,ub)
```
Maybe tolerance options unsupported? HiGHS options in scipy: `'presolve'`, `'dual_feasibility_tolerance'`, `'primal_feasibility_tolerance'` are valid? I think yes. To be safe, only pass `{'presolve': True}`. Unsupported may raise. Use just options={'presolve': True}. Could omit options.

`lp_one_step(f)`:
```
def lp_one_step(f, ub=1.0):
   g=lp_solve_one(f,ub)
   if g is None: return None, inf
   M=max_conv_array(g)
   if not finite or M<=0: return None,inf
   h=g/math.sqrt(M)
   # candidate list
   cands=[h]
   for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
       cands.append((1-t)*f + t*h)
   best=None; best_sc=inf
   for cand in cands:
       sc=score_array(cand)
       if sc<best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best=cand.copy()
   if best is None or not finite(best_sc): return None,inf
   # normalize best to M=1 for possible iteration? Return raw best? We can return normalized.
   best_n,_=normalize_M1(best)
   return best_n, min(score_array(best_n), best_sc)
```

But h from LP is scaled M=1; f is M=1. If blend has M>1, score_array handles. Normalizing best_n after picking best may alter the particular blend? It has same score. For next LP, anchor M=1. Good.

def resample_sequence(a,n):
   if n<=0: return []
   old=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if old.size==n: return old.copy()
   if old.size==0: return central_seq(n)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,old.size)
   y=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(y,x,old)
   b=np.clip(b,0,1000)
   if b.sum()<1e-9: b=central_seq(n)
   return b

```
def coordinate_try(a,c,s,i):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; x=a[i]; k2=2*i
    base=c.copy(); lv=np.zeros(L)
    idx=i+np.arange(n)
    lv[idx]=2.0*a
    if k2<L: lv[k2]=0.0
    base[idx] -= x*lv[idx]
    if k2<L: base[k2] -= x*x
    amax=float(c.max?) no, a.max
    cands=coordinate_candidates(x, amax, 5.0)
    if not cands: return None
    Y=np.array(cands,dtype=float)
    # candidate that equals x may be among; if x outside? okay
    term=base[None,:]+Y[:,None]*lv[None,:]
    Mlin=term.max(axis=1)
    if k2<L: Mvals=np.maximum(Mlin, base[k2]+Y*Y)
    else: Mvals=Mlin
    Svals=s-x+Y
    valid=Svals>1e-12
    if not np.any(valid): return None
    F=np.full(Y.shape,np.inf)
    Cv=2.0*n
    F[valid]=Cv*Mvals[valid]/(Svals[valid]*Svals[valid])
    best_idx=int(np.argmin(F))
    if not math.isfinite(F[best_idx]): return None
    old_score=Cv*float(c.max())/(s*s)
    if F[best_idx] < old_score - 1e-11:
        y=float(Y[best_idx])
        new_c=base + y*lv
        if k2<L: new_c[k2]+=y*y
        if new_c.size and new_c.min() < 0: new_c=np.maximum(new_c,0.0)
        new_s=floats-s-x+y
        new_score=float(Cv*new_c.max()/(new_s*new_s))
        return y,new_c,new_s,new_score
    return None
```

def noisy_start(a,rng,sigma):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); n=a.size
   noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, n))
   # smooth noise with small window
   k=5; noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
   a *= noise
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   return a
```

Detailed `lp_search_length`:
```
def lp_search_length(n, deadline, rng, global_best):
    if n < 2 or linprog is None:
        return None, float('inf')
    pool=[]
    # central
    a=power_sequence? central
    add_start(...)
    # global best resampled
    if global_best is not None: add_start(resample_sequence(global_best,n))
    # height sequence maybe global? Could try global height_sequence_1 but inside? use try
    for ... power starts add
    # noisy from best pool if possible
    if not pool: central
    pool.sort(...)
    local_best=None; local_score=inf
    queue=[b for _,b in pool]
    # We'll maintain a pointer to use local first, then queue.
    attempts=0
    max_attempts = 12 if n<=384 else (10 if n==512 else 4)
    no_improve=0
    consecutive=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10 and attempts < max_attempts:
        if local_best is None:
            if not queue: break
            anchor=queue.pop(0)
        else:
            if consecutive == 0:
                anchor=local_best
            else:
                if not queue:
                    # generate noisy from local
                    noisy = noisy_start(local_best, rng, 0.2)
                    nb,sc=normalize_and_score(noisy)
                    if nb is None: break
                    queue.append(nb); no_improve=0; consecutive=0; continue
                anchor=queue.pop(0)
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: break
        cand, sc = lp_one_step(anchor)
        attempts +=1
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score - 1e-12:
            local_best = cand
            local_score = sc
            consecutive = 0
            # add noisy variants
            for sig in (0.08, 0.2):
                nb, nsc = normalize_and_score(noisy_start(local_best, rng, sig))
                if nb is not None:
                    queue.append(nb)
                    if len(queue)>10: queue.pop()
            print(f"LP n={n} iter={attempts} local={local_score:.10f}")
        else:
            consecutive += 1
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > max_attempts: break
    if local_best is None and pool:
       local_best=pool[0][1]; local_score=pool[0][0]
    return local_best, local_score
```
Need if local_best improved, `consecutive=0`; if no improve, consecutive increments; after consecutive >0, it pops queue. Good.

def normalize_and_score(a):

</think>

```python
"""
Optimizer for the nonnegative convolution-peak factor

    score(a) = 2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Main ideas
----------
1. Keep a sequence normalized so max(convolve(a, a)) == 1.  Then the score is
   2*n / sum(a)^2, so improving the score is equivalent to increasing the sum.

2. Use an LP outer-approximation / state-of-the-art idea:
   For a current normalized feasible-ish sequence f with f*f <= 1, solve

       maximize 1^T g
       subject to (f * g)_k <= 1 for k = 0..2n-2,
                  0 <= g <= 1.

   The LP solution g is scaled so max(g*g) == 1.  Blend it with f and keep any
   point that really lowers the exact score.  Repeat with the improved point and
   with a few noisy/reshaped anchors.  This is the central new search engine.

3. Polish the best sequence with a fast exact coordinate descent on the homogeneous
   score, and with a short anchored L-BFGS-B run using a log-sum-exp approximation.

4. Always retain valid fallbacks (central-binomial / edge-flat sequences), finish
   with validation, and return a plain Python list of nonnegative floats.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None

TARGET_UPPER_BOUND = 1.5027
COEFF_UB = 1000.0


def central_seq(n):
    """Truncated coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), a strong baseline."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (i + 0.5) / (i + 1.0)
    return a


def power_sequence(n, alpha, beta):
    """Simple edge/tail shape: left decay and optional right growth."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = (x + 1.0) ** (-float(alpha))
    if abs(float(beta)) > 1e-12:
        right = (n - x) ** (-float(beta))
        left = left * right
    return left


def convolve_full(arr):
    """Full convolution, direct for moderate length and FFT for very long ones."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 2048:
        c = np.convolve(arr, arr, mode="full")
    else:
        N = 1
        while N < L:
            N <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(arr, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
        c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def max_conv_array(arr):
    """Maximum coefficient of convolution(a, a), after evaluator-style clipping."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    c = convolve_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    return m if math.isfinite(m) else float("inf")


def score_array(arr):
    """Exact objective compatible with evaluate_sequence, but without I/O."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = convolve_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def normalize_M1(a):
    """Scale a sequence so max(convolve(a,a)) == 1.  Returns (scaled, score)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    m = max_conv_array(a)
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")
    b = a / math.sqrt(m)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    if np.max(b) > COEFF_UB:
        b = b * (COEFF_UB / float(np.max(b)))
    sc = score_array(b)
    return b, sc


def normalize_and_score(a):
    b, sc = normalize_M1(a)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        sc = score_array(b)
    return b, sc


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    """Linspace resample a sequence to length n, with safe fallback."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return central_seq(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(new, old, a)
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    if float(np.sum(b)) < 1e-9:
        b = central_seq(n)
    return b


def noisy_start(a, rng, sigma=0.1):
    """Low-pass smoothed random positive multiplier."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), n))
    if n > 5:
        kernel = np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
    a = a * noise
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)


def add_starts(pool, sequences):
    """Normalize candidate sequences and append finite scored ones to pool."""
    for a in sequences:
        b, sc = normalize_and_score(a)
        if b is not None and math.isfinite(sc):
            pool.append((sc, b))


def build_conv_matrix(f):
    """Dense matrix C where C[k, j] = f[k-j] when valid for linear convolution."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = f.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(L):
        lo = 0 if k < n else k - n + 1
        hi = k if k < n else n - 1
        if lo <= hi:
            A[k, lo:hi + 1] = f[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
    return A


def lp_solve_anchor(f, ub=1.0):
    """Solve max sum(g) s.t. f*g <= 1, 0 <= g <= ub."""
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if f.size == 0:
        return None
    try:
        A = build_conv_matrix(f)
    except Exception:
        return None
    n = f.size
    try:
        res = linprog(
            -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=[(0.0, float(ub))] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or res.x is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=ub, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(g, 0.0, float(ub))


def lp_one_step(f):
    """
    One LP outer-approximation move.

    f should be normalized to M(f) == max(f*f) == 1.  Returns the best exact-score
    candidate from LP blends, normalized to M == 1, and its exact score.
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = lp_solve_anchor(f, ub=1.0)
    if g is None or g.size != f.size:
        return None, float("inf")
    if float(np.sum(g)) <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    m_g = max_conv_array(g)
    if not math.isfinite(m_g) or m_g <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    h = g / math.sqrt(m_g)

    cands = []
    cands.append(h)
    cands.append(g)
    for t in (0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0):
        cands.append((1.0 - t) * f + t * h)
        cands.append((1.0 - t) * f + t * g)

    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    for cand in cands:
        sc = score_array(cand)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)

    if best is None or not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        return None, float("inf")
    bn, scn = normalize_M1(best)
    if not math.isfinite(scn):
        return None, float("inf")
    return bn, scn


def lp_search_length(n, deadline, rng, global_best):
    """Search one length using repeated LP moves from several anchors."""
    if n < 2 or n > 1200:
        return None, float("inf")
    if linprog is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    pool = []
    starts = [central_seq(n)]

    if global_best is not None and np.asarray(global_best).size > 0:
        starts.append(resample_sequence(global_best, n))

    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if hs.size > 0:
            starts.append(resample_sequence(hs, n))
    except Exception:
        pass

    for alpha, beta in ((0.5, 0.0), (0.45, 0.05), (0.5, 0.10), (0.40, 0.10), (0.30, 0.00)):
        starts.append(power_sequence(n, alpha, beta))

    if pool:
        pass
    add_starts(pool, starts)

    if not pool:
        fallback = central_seq(n)
        bf, sf = normalize_M1(fallback)
        pool.append((sf, bf))

    pool.sort(key=lambda pair: pair[0])
    queue = [pair[1] for pair in pool]
    if queue:
        local_best = queue[0].copy()
        local_score = pool[0][0]
    else:
        return None, float("inf")

    max_attempts = 18 if n <= 384 else (14 if n <= 512 else 7 if n <= 768 else 4)
    attempts = 0
    consecutive_no = 0
    spent_no = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 25 and attempts < max_attempts:
        if local_best is None or consecutive_no == 0:
            anchor = local_best
        else:
            if not queue:
                noisy = noisy_start(local_best, rng, 0.15)
                nb, ns = normalize_and_score(noisy)
                if nb is None or not math.isfinite(ns):
                    break
                queue.append(nb)
                consecutive_no = 0
                continue
            anchor = queue.pop(0)

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 35:
            break

        cand, sc = lp_one_step(anchor)
        attempts += 1

        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score - 1e-12:
            local_best = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
            local_score = float(sc)
            consecutive_no = 0
            spent_no = 0
            print(f"LP n={n} iter={attempts} local_score={local_score:.10f}")

            for sigma in (0.07, 0.18):
                noisy = noisy_start(local_best, rng, sigma)
                nb, ns = normalize_and_score(noisy)
                if nb is not None and math.isfinite(ns):
                    queue.append(nb)
                    if len(queue) > 12:
                        queue.pop(0)
        else:
            consecutive_no += 1
            spent_no += 1
            if spent_no > max_attempts:
                break

    if local_best is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    final_sc = score_array(local_best)
    if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
        final_sc = local_score
    return local_best, final_sc


def coordinate_candidates(x, amax, high):
    """A compact geometric/angular candidate set for a coordinate change."""
    if not math.isfinite(x):
        x = 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(amax):
        amax = 1.0
    high = float(max(5.0, 2.0 * max(x, amax), 1.5 * x if x > 0 else 0.0))
    high = min(high, 1.0e4)

    raw = [0.0, 1e-5, 1e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 1e-1, 25e-2, 5e-1, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0]
    if x > 0.0:
        raw.extend([x, 0.5 * x, 2.0 * x])
    if amax > 0.0:
        raw.extend([0.5 * amax, amax])

    out = []
    for v in raw:
        if not math.isfinite(v) or v < 0.0 or v > high:
            continue
        ok = True
        for u in out:
            if abs(v - u) <= 1e-12 * (1.0 + max(abs(v), abs(u))):
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            out.append(float(v))
    out.sort()
    if not out:
        out = [0.0, min(1.0, high)]
    return out


def coordinate_try(a, c, s, score, i):
    """Try changing one coordinate, exactly maximizing/minimizing the exact score on a grid."""
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    x = float(a[i])
    k2 = 2 * i

    base = c.copy()
    lv = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = i + np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)

    lv[idx] = 2.0 * a
    if 0 <= k2 < L:
        lv[k2] = 0.0

    base[idx] -= x * lv[idx]
    if 0 <= k2 < L:
        base[k2] -= x * x

    amax = float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 1.0
    cands = coordinate_candidates(x, amax, 5.0)
    Y = np.asarray(cands, dtype=np.float64)
    if Y.size == 0:
        return None

    term = base[None, :] + Y[:, None] * lv[None, :]
    Mlin = term.max(axis=1)
    if 0 <= k2 < L:
        Mvals = np.maximum(Mlin, base[k2] + Y * Y)
    else:
        Mvals = Mlin

    Svals = s - x + Y
    valid = Svals > 1e-12
    if not np.any(valid):
        return None

    C = 2.0 * float(n)
    F = np.full(Y.shape, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
    F[valid] = C * Mvals[valid] / (Svals[valid] * Svals[valid])

    best_idx = int(np.argmin(F))
    if not math.isfinite(F[best_idx]):
        return None
    if F[best_idx] >= score - 1e-12:
        return None

    y = float(Y[best_idx])
    new_c = base + y * lv
    if 0 <= k2 < L:
        new_c[k2] += y * y
    new_c = np.nan_to_num(new_c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if new_c.size and float(new_c.min()) < 0.0:
        new_c = np.maximum(new_c, 0.0)
    new_s = float(s - x + y)
    if new_s <= 0.0:
        return None
    Mnew = float(new_c.max())
    new_score = float(C * Mnew / (new_s * new_s))
    if not math.isfinite(new_score):
        return None
    return (y, new_c, new_s, new_score)


def coordinate_polish(a, budget_s, rng=None):
    """Exact one-coordinate descent on the score, with periodic M-normalization."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1200 or budget_s <= 0.5:
        return a, score_array(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.5, float(budget_s)) - 0.25

    a, _ = normalize_M1(a)
    c = convolve_full(a)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if M <= 0.0 or s < 1e-8 or not math.isfinite(M):
        fb = central_seq(min(n, 512))
        return fb, score_array(fb)

    score = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        score = score_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    sweep = 0
    no_improve_streak = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        sweep += 1
        order = np.arange(n)
        rng.shuffle(order)
        improved = False

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            res = coordinate_try(a, c, s, score, int(i))
            if res is not None:
                y, new_c, new_s, new_score = res
                a[int(i)] = float(y)
                c = new_c
                s = new_s
                score = new_score
                improved = True
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    no_improve_streak = 0

        if not improved:
            no_improve_streak += 1
            if no_improve_streak >= 2:
                break

        # Keep the sequence at M == 1 whenever possible (score is invariant).
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else float("inf")
        if math.isfinite(M) and M > 0.0 and abs(M - 1.0) > 1e-7:
            alpha = 1.0 / math.sqrt(M)
            a = a * alpha
            c = c / M
            s = s * alpha
            M = 1.0
            score = float(2.0 * n / (s * s)) if s > 0 else float("inf")

    final_score = score_array(best_a)
    return best_a, final_score


def split_vector(a, anchor):
    """Remove the anchor coordinate from a vector."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor == 0:
        return a[1:].copy()
    if anchor == n - 1:
        return a[:-1].copy()
    x = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    x[:anchor] = a[:anchor]
    x[anchor:] = a[anchor + 1:]
    return x


def join_vector(x, anchor, anchor_value=1.0):
    """Insert anchor_value at anchor and fill the rest from x."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size + 1
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    if anchor == 0:
        a[0] = float(anchor_value)
        if n > 1:
            a[1:] = x
    elif anchor == n - 1:
        if n > 1:
            a[:-1] = x
        a[-1] = float(anchor_value)
    else:
        if anchor > 0:
            a[:anchor] = x[:anchor]
        a[anchor] = float(anchor_value)
        rest = n - anchor - 1
        if rest > 0:
            a[anchor + 1:] = x[anchor:anchor + rest]
    return a


def smooth_obj_anchored(x, n, anchor, beta):
    """
    Smooth log-sum-exp approximation with analytic gradient.

    The full vector has a[anchor] = 1.  x contains the other coordinates in
    ascending-index order.
    """
    n = int(n)
    anchor = int(anchor)
    if n <= 1:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor < 0 or anchor >= n:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n - 1:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, COEFF_UB)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor == 0:
        a[0] = 1.0
        a[1:] = x
    elif anchor == n - 1:
        a[:-1] = x
        a[-1] = 1.0
    else:
        a[:anchor] = x[:anchor]
        a[anchor] = 1.0
        a[anchor + 1:] = x[anchor:]
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)

    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not math.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    z = np.minimum(beta * (c - m), 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    great = float(np.sum(ez))
    if great <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(great):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    L = m + math.log(great) / beta
    w = ez / great

    conv = np.convolve(a, w[::-1])
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    h = conv[2 * n - 2 - idx]
    dL = 2.0 * h

    C = 2.0 * float(n)
    G = C * (dL - (2.0 * L * a) / (s * s))
    val = C * L / (s * s)

    if anchor == 0:
        grad = G[1:]
    elif anchor == n - 1:
        grad = G[:-1]
    else:
        grad = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        grad[:anchor] = G[:anchor]
        grad[anchor:] = G[anchor + 1:]

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, grad
    return float(val), grad


def lbfgs_polish(a, budget_s):
    """Short anchored L-BFGS-B refinement using the smooth log-sum-exp objective."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
    n = a.size
    if minimize is None or n < 2 or n > 512 or budget_s <= 2.0:
        return a, score_array(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 0.5

    a, base_score = normalize_M1(a)
    if not math.isfinite(base_score):
        return central_seq(min(n, 512)), score_array(central_seq(min(n, 512)))

    anchor = int(np.argmax(a))
    val = float(a[anchor])
    if not math.isfinite(val) or val <= 1e-8:
        anchor = int(n // 2)
        val = float(a[anchor])
        if val <= 1e-8:
            return a, base_score

    a_scaled = a / val
    x0 = split_vector(a_scaled, anchor)
    ub = max(3.0, 2.5 * float(np.max(x0)) + 1.0)
    x0 = np.nan_to_num(x0, nan=0.0, posinf=ub, neginf=0.0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, 0.0, ub)

    best_x = x0.copy()
    best_score = base_score

    if n <= 128:
        stages = ((20.0, 120), (60.0, 70), (150.0, 40))
    else:
        stages = ((20.0, 70), (60.0, 40), (150.0, 24))

    bounds = [(0.0, ub)] * x0.size

    for beta, mit in stages:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun=smooth_obj_anchored,
                x0=best_x,
                args=(n, anchor, float(beta)),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(mit),
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            xx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            break

        if xx.size != x0.size:
            continue
        xx = np.nan_to_num(xx, nan=0.0, posinf=ub, neginf=0.0)
        xx = np.clip(xx, 0.0, ub)
        full = join_vector(xx, anchor, 1.0)
        full = np.nan_to_num(full, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
        full = np.clip(full, 0.0, COEFF_UB)
        sc = score_array(full)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_x = xx.copy()
            best_score = sc
            best_a = full

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0 and n <= 24:
        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun=lambda z: score_array(join_vector(z, anchor, 1.0)),
                x0=best_x,
                method="Powell",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": 600, "xtol": 1e-9, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
            )
            xx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if xx.size == best_x.size:
                xx = np.clip(xx, 0.0, ub)
                full = join_vector(xx, anchor, 1.0)
                sc = score_array(full)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_x = xx.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = full
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        best_full = join_vector(best_x, anchor, 1.0)
    except Exception:
        best_full = a_scaled
    best_full, sc = normalize_M1(best_full)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return a, score_array(a)
    return best_full, sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400):
    """
    Search for a best sequence within the time budget.

    Returns a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 42
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_int)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total - 1.5

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Baselines: central-binomial / edge-flat at several lengths.
    for n in (64, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        a = central_seq(n)
        sc = score_array(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    # Use the supplied previous construction if it exists and is valid.
    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if hs.size > 0:
            sc = score_array(hs)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = hs.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # A few analytic edge shapes can provide safer anchors for LP.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        for n in (384, 512, 768, 1024):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                break
            for alpha, beta in ((0.5, 0.0), (0.45, 0.08), (0.5, 0.12), (0.40, 0.12)):
                a = power_sequence(n, alpha, beta)
                sc = score_array(a)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()

    print(f"initial best_score={best_score:.10f} n={0 if best_a is None else best_a.size}")

    # LP outer-approximation / state-of-the-art moves at selected lengths.
    lp_lengths = (512, 1024, 384, 768)
    for n in lp_lengths:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break
        if n == 1024 and remaining < 180.0:
            continue
        if n == 768 and remaining < 120.0:
            continue
        if n == 512 and remaining < 80.0:
            continue
        if n == 384 and remaining < 45.0:
            continue

        try:
            la, ls = lp_search_length(
                n,
                deadline,
                rng,
                best_a if best_a is not None else central_seq(n),
            )
        except Exception:
            la, ls = None, float("inf")

        if la is not None and math.isfinite(ls):
            sc = score_array(la)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = la.copy()
                print(f"after LP n={n} best_score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Smooth local polish for medium lengths.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size >= 2 and best_a.size <= 512:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_score > TARGET_UPPER_BOUND + 1e-9 and remaining > 70.0:
            budget_l = min(remaining - 2.0, 55.0)
            if budget_l > 5.0:
                try:
                    la, ls = lbfgs_polish(best_a, budget_l)
                except Exception:
                    la, ls = best_a, best_score
                if la is not None and math.isfinite(ls):
                    sc = score_array(la)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = la.copy()
                        print(f"after L-BFGS best_score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Exact coordinate polish near the end.  This is reliable and often helps LP
    # outputs by re-balancing active convolution peaks.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size >= 2 and best_a.size <= 1200:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 15.0:
            if best_a.size <= 384:
                budget_c = min(remaining - 2.0, 70.0)
            elif best_a.size <= 768:
                budget_c = min(remaining - 2.0, 45.0)
            else:
                budget_c = min(remaining - 2.0, 25.0)
            if budget_c > 3.0:
                try:
                    pa, ps = coordinate_polish(best_a, budget_c, rng)
                except Exception:
                    pa, ps = best_a, best_score
                if pa is not None and math.isfinite(ps):
                    sc = score_array(pa)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = pa.copy()
                        print(f"after coordinate polish best_score={best_score:.10f}")

    # One final very short LP attempt on the current best length if there is time.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size >= 2 and best_a.size <= 1024:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_score > TARGET_UPPER_BOUND + 1e-9 and remaining > 90.0:
            nfinal = int(best_a.size)
            try:
                fa, _ = normalize_M1(best_a)
                if fa is not None:
                    la, ls = lp_one_step(fa)
                    if la is not None and math.isfinite(ls):
                        sc = score_array(la)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_a = la.copy()
                            print(f"final LP best_score={best_score:.10f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final clean-up and validation.
    try:
        best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        best_a = central_seq(512)
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=COEFF_UB, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, COEFF_UB)

    if best_a.size == 0 or float(np.sum(best_a)) < 0.01 or not math.isfinite(score_array(best_a)):
        best_a = central_seq(512)
        best_score = score_array(best_a)

    # Optional authoritative check, without importing it.
    try:
        ext = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in best_a]))
        if math.isfinite(ext) and ext < best_score:
            best_score = ext
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL best_score={best_score:.10f} n={best_a.size}")
    return [float(v) for v in best_a]
```
